# 6. Adjudication Rules

A candidate passage may receive one of five classifications:

| Classification | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Direct | The artifact expressly establishes the bounded claim. |
| Indirect | The artifact contributes support but does not independently establish the claim. |
| Qualifying | The artifact narrows or conditions the claim. |
| Contradictory | The artifact conflicts with the claim or frozen architecture. |
| Not Relevant | The textual match does not carry evidentiary weight for the claim. |

No claim may advance to validated status solely because matching text was found.
